Isagoge ad magisterium iudiciorum astrorum
Al-Qabīsī (Alcabitius) (1521)
========================================================================

PROVENANCE NOTE (Tower of Babel staging): Al-Qabīsī's Isagoge (Introductorius) with the commentary of John of Saxony, Latin, Venice 1521 (Biblioteca Nazionale Centrale di Firenze; via ProQuest Early European Books). No public-domain English translation exists; the Latin text is reproduced as a documentary edition. This 1521 edition is in the public domain.

========================================================================
